These include the group of teaching assistants called researchers. In Article 33 of Higher Education Law 2547, the job descriptions of researchers were made clear: "Investigators are teaching assistants who assist in research, study and experiments at higher education institutions and perform other relevant tasks given by competent bodies." Although this law clause does not specify clearly and clearly, it is the most common case in the practice where research is the basis of university education membership and where teaching is usually started from the task of research. In institutes established for grad school abroad, researchers in universities are trained through grad school in accordance with Articles 33, 35, and Articles 2547 of Law abroad. On February 23, 1918, Numan Çelebi Jahan was the first president of the People's Republic of Crimean. ==Early life==Numan Çelebi Jahan studied in İstanbul, founded the Tatar Youth Organization while studying at Vefa High School, and founded the Crimean Tatar Student Union and Country in 1917. He later became the first president of the People's Republic of Crimean.
